### Mangaluru

#### New MUDA chairman to get State nod for draft Master Plan III on priority

**In short:** Newly nominated MUDA chairman Santhosh Kumar Shetty said on Monday his first priority was getting government approval for the draft Master Plan III for the Mangaluru Local Planning Area, submitted in January and awaiting nod for over seven months; Master Plan II came into effect October 1, 2009, and Plan III should have been in place by 2019. He will discuss it with Urban Development Minister Yatindra Siddaramaiah and District In-charge Minister U.T.

Newly nominated MUDA chairman Santhosh Kumar Shetty said on Monday his first priority was getting government approval for the draft Master Plan III for the Mangaluru Local Planning Area, submitted in January and awaiting nod for over seven months; Master Plan II came into effect October 1, 2009, and Plan III should have been in place by 2019. He will discuss it with Urban Development Minister Yatindra Siddaramaiah and District In-charge Minister U.T. Khader; once approved, public objections will be invited, and he said MUDA would take up infrastructure beyond MCC jurisdiction in areas such as Surathkal and Bajpe, explore funding for an underpass or flyover, and consider buying private land for parks. Of 200 housing sites on 18 acres at Kunjathbail, 35 have been distributed; 142 sites on 13.5 acres at Konaje are yet to be distributed; work has begun on 700 sites on 45 acres at Chelairu.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** New MUDA chairman to get State nod for draft Master Plan III on priority
- **Told:** Newly nominated MUDA chairman Santhosh Kumar Shetty said on Monday his first priority was getting government approval for the draft Master Plan III for the Mangaluru Local Planning Area, submitted in January and awaiting nod for over seven months; Master Plan II came into effect October 1, 2009, and Plan III should have been in place by 2019.
- **Purpose:** Of 200 housing sites on 18 acres at Kunjathbail, 35 have been distributed; 142 sites on 13.5 acres at Konaje are yet to be distributed; work has begun on 700 sites on 45 acres at Chelairu.

**Important points**
- Khader.
- once approved, public objections will be invited, and he said MUDA would take up infrastructure beyond MCC jurisdiction in areas such as Surathkal and Bajpe, explore funding for an underpass or flyover, and consider buying private land for parks.
- Of 200 housing sites on 18 acres at Kunjathbail, 35 have been distributed.
- 142 sites on 13.5 acres at Konaje are yet to be distributed.
- work has begun on 700 sites on 45 acres at Chelairu.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.4; The Hindu Mangaluru p.7_

#### Man stabs brother to death following fight over mobile phone

**In short:** Adiveppa Kumbara (41), a daily-wage labourer from Belagavi district, was allegedly stabbed in the chest and killed by his younger brother Raju Kumbara near Kottara Cross on Sunday night following a quarrel over a mobile phone, Police Commissioner Sudheer Kumar Reddy C H said. The brothers, both alcohol-dependent and living with their mother Eravva in a rented house for about a year, had fought after Adiveppa took Raju's phone at their mother's petty shop; Adiveppa was declared dead at Wenlock Hospital after the assault around 10.45 pm.

Adiveppa Kumbara (41), a daily-wage labourer from Belagavi district, was allegedly stabbed in the chest and killed by his younger brother Raju Kumbara near Kottara Cross on Sunday night following a quarrel over a mobile phone, Police Commissioner Sudheer Kumar Reddy C H said. The brothers, both alcohol-dependent and living with their mother Eravva in a rented house for about a year, had fought after Adiveppa took Raju's phone at their mother's petty shop; Adiveppa was declared dead at Wenlock Hospital after the assault around 10.45 pm. The Urwa Police have arrested Raju and registered a case under section 103(1) of the BNS.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Man stabs brother to death following fight over mobile phone

**In short:** The Dakshina Kannada District Youth Congress Committee organised a district-level information workshop of the Bharat Jodo Yuva Sangha at the District Congress Office on Monday, chaired by District Youth Congress President Ibrahim Nawaz. The workshop focused on strengthening the Youth Congress organisation, increasing young workers' participation and chalking out organisational and public-oriented programmes.

#### 'Have no moral right'

**In short:** Karnataka State Commission for Backward Classes member Prathibha Kulai said those who organised Puttur Chalo had no morality to invite her to the programme, speaking to mediapersons in Mangaluru on Monday. She asked Hindu leaders who held the march to ensure the marriage of a young woman who became pregnant and is now the mother of a child with the son of a BJP leader from Puttur.

#### INTACH organises Hindustani vocal recital

**In short:** The Indian National Trust for Art and Cultural Heritage (INTACH) Mangaluru Chapter and the Art Kanara Trust organised the eighth edition of the Baithak at Kodialguthu in the Musical Heritage of Karnataka series, featuring a Hindustani vocal recital by Neeharika Deraje at the Kodialguthu Centre for Art & Culture, Ballalbagh. Neeharika was accompanied by Shrivathsa Sharma on tabla, Medha G Bhat on harmonium and Samveda G Bhat on tanpura; she opened with Raag Puriya Dhanashree and also presented compositions in Raag Durga.

#### Slew of events to mark 'Alzheimer's Month' celebrations starting today

**In short:** People's Association for Geriatric Empowerment (PAGE) will observe Alzheimer's Month throughout September with memory screening programmes, awareness activities and competitions for senior citizens, launching celebrations at Kadri Park on September 1 at 7 am. Dr Prabha Adhikari, PAGE vice-president, said early diagnosis can delay dementia progression and that India is estimated to have nearly 8.8 million people living with dementia, over 90% undiagnosed; Yenepoya Medical College will conduct free memory, blood pressure and blood sugar tests at Kadri Park on September 1 from 7.30 am to 10 am.

People's Association for Geriatric Empowerment (PAGE) will observe Alzheimer's Month throughout September with memory screening programmes, awareness activities and competitions for senior citizens, launching celebrations at Kadri Park on September 1 at 7 am. Dr Prabha Adhikari, PAGE vice-president, said early diagnosis can delay dementia progression and that India is estimated to have nearly 8.8 million people living with dementia, over 90% undiagnosed; Yenepoya Medical College will conduct free memory, blood pressure and blood sugar tests at Kadri Park on September 1 from 7.30 am to 10 am. Competitions for seniors will be held at Rotary Club Mangalore North on World Alzheimer's Day, September 21, from 2 pm to 6 pm, with the grand finale at Town Hall.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Slew of events to mark 'Alzheimer's Month' celebrations starting today
- **Told:** People's Association for Geriatric Empowerment (PAGE) will observe Alzheimer's Month throughout September with memory screening programmes, awareness activities and competitions for senior citizens, launching celebrations at Kadri Park on September 1 at 7 am.

#### Auto fares hiked in Mangaluru from today

**In short:** Autorickshaw fares will go up in Mangaluru on Tuesday, with the minimum for the first 1.5 km rising from ₹35 to ₹40 and every subsequent kilometre from ₹15 to ₹20, after the RTA headed by Dakshina Kannada Deputy Commissioner H.V. Darshan approved the revision in an order on Friday citing rising costs of fuel, CNG, and spare parts.

Autorickshaw fares will go up in Mangaluru on Tuesday, with the minimum for the first 1.5 km rising from ₹35 to ₹40 and every subsequent kilometre from ₹15 to ₹20, after the RTA headed by Dakshina Kannada Deputy Commissioner H.V. Darshan approved the revision in an order on Friday citing rising costs of fuel, CNG, and spare parts. There will be no waiting charge for the first 10 minutes and ₹5 for every subsequent 15 minutes; between 10 p.m. and 5 a.m. drivers may collect 1.5 times the normal fare, and the revised chart must be displayed with meters recalibrated. Dakshina Kannada is now on a par with Udupi and Shivamogga (₹40 and ₹20 from September 14, 2022 and November 14, 2022); previous DK fares of ₹35 and ₹17 per subsequent km had been in effect since October 27, 2022.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Auto fares hiked in Mangaluru from today

- **Purpose:** Dakshina Kannada is now on a par with Udupi and Shivamogga (₹40 and ₹20 from September 14, 2022 and November 14, 2022); previous DK fares of ₹35 and ₹17 per subsequent km had been in effect since October 27, 2022.

**Important points**

#### Cabinet meeting in Mangaluru on Sept 18

**In short:** The Karnataka government has decided to hold a State Cabinet meeting in Mangaluru on September 18, with proposals concerning the coastal districts likely to receive special priority. Chief Secretary Shalini Rajneesh has directed Additional Chief Secretaries, Principal Secretaries and Secretaries of all government departments to immediately prepare coastal-district proposals and place them before the Cabinet in the form of Cabinet notes.

#### MCC body makes 4-way waste segregation a must

**In short:** The Mangaluru City Corporation has made segregation of waste into wet, dry, sanitary and special-care categories mandatory at source for residents and all bulk waste generators, giving citizens one month to comply before collection staff refuse improperly segregated waste. Wet and sanitary waste will be collected daily, dry and special-care waste on Fridays, and violations will attract penalties under the MCC Solid Waste Management Bye-laws, 2019.

### Coastal Karnataka

#### Class 9 student ends life for scoring less marks in exam

**In short:** Shravan (14) of Kokkada allegedly died by suicide on Sunday after being upset over scoring three out of 40 marks in Hindi, his uncle Sathish Gowda K told the Dharmasthala Police in a complaint filed on Monday. Shravan's father had attended a parent-teacher meeting at the private school on Saturday, where a teacher informed him of the low score; the boy reportedly locked himself in his study room and was found dead later.

The Dharmasthala Police have registered a case.

#### Hindu activists allege PFI's involvement in Puttur clash; demand thorough probe

**In short:** The Vishva Hindu Parishad, Bajrang Dal and Hindu Jagarana Vedike, Puttur unit, staged Puttur Chalo on Monday demanding withdrawal of the case registered against the owner of a digital service centre in Darbe and a thorough probe into document-damage incidents. Protesters alleged banned PFI activists were involved in the clash and demanded FIRs against them under the Karnataka Control of Organised Crime Act, action against police for allegedly delaying case registration, and arrest of those who entered a police jeep and obstructed personnel.

#### ICAR–CPCRI to celebrate World Coconut Day tomorrow

**In short:** World Coconut Day 2026 will be celebrated at the ICAR–Central Plantation Crops Research Institute (ICAR–CPCRI), Kasaragod, on September 2, with the theme 'Coconuts in Times of Uncertainty: Securing Food, Energy, and Livelihoods.' ICAR–CPCRI will launch SHITIZ 2.0, an online entrepreneur mentorship and pre-incubation programme, and inaugurate a two-day stakeholders' workshop on management of extreme climate vulnerability in plantation crops. Dr Dinakar Adiga, Director, ICAR–Directorate of Cashew Research, Puttur, will inaugurate the celebrations; outstanding farmers and entrepreneurs will be felicitated and MoUs signed to promote technology commercialisation in the plantation sector.

#### Alva's student gets Rs 46.33-lakh job package

**In short:** Srushti Manjunath Ullagaddi, a final-year Information Science Engineering student at Alva's Institute of Engineering and Technology (AIET), has been selected as a Software Engineer by Visa with annual compensation and quantified benefits totalling Rs 46.33 lakh. Her base annual compensation is Rs 35.58 lakh, with family medical insurance worth Rs 7.5 lakh, OPD benefit of Rs 25,000 and education subsidy up to Rs 3 lakh; a 10-week Visa internship during her sixth semester with a Rs 90,000 stipend led to the full-time offer.

Srushti Manjunath Ullagaddi, a final-year Information Science Engineering student at Alva's Institute of Engineering and Technology (AIET), has been selected as a Software Engineer by Visa with annual compensation and quantified benefits totalling Rs 46.33 lakh. Her base annual compensation is Rs 35.58 lakh, with family medical insurance worth Rs 7.5 lakh, OPD benefit of Rs 25,000 and education subsidy up to Rs 3 lakh; a 10-week Visa internship during her sixth semester with a Rs 90,000 stipend led to the full-time offer. Srushti, from Hattimattur village in Haveri district, is the daughter of farmer Manjunath and homemaker Sujatha.

#### Puttur Cong MLA writes to CM, alleges corruption in KRIDL

**In short:** Puttur MLA Ashok Kumar Rai has urged Chief Minister D K Shivakumar to order an investigation into allegations of corruption, illegal collection of money and threats to contractors by officials of the Karnataka Rural Infrastructure Development Limited (KRIDL), Mangaluru division, in a letter dated August 25. Rai alleged KRIDL Mangaluru Executive Engineer Vijay and Junior Engineer Prajwal collected additional amounts of up to seven per cent from contractors in the name of a "KRIDL charge" and that contractors were told monthly payments had to be made to the district in-charge and department ministers.

#### Bhatkal hospital to get mother & child care block

**In short:** Karnataka Health and Family Welfare, Minority Welfare, Haj and Wakf Minister U.T. Khader said a dedicated Mother and Child Care block is proposed at Bhatkal Taluk Hospital to improve healthcare and ease pressure on the existing facility, which has a sanctioned capacity of 100 beds but often admits more than 130 patients.

Karnataka Health and Family Welfare, Minority Welfare, Haj and Wakf Minister U.T. Khader said a dedicated Mother and Child Care block is proposed at Bhatkal Taluk Hospital to improve healthcare and ease pressure on the existing facility, which has a sanctioned capacity of 100 beds but often admits more than 130 patients. Khader said steps would be taken to establish a separate MCC unit if the hospital records more than 100 deliveries a month, and that a meeting with MLA Mankal Vaidya and senior officials would be held soon to finalise plans; he also said the foundation stone for a Haj Bhavan in Mangaluru would be laid soon.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Bhatkal hospital to get mother & child care block
- **Told:** Karnataka Health and Family Welfare, Minority Welfare, Haj and Wakf Minister U.T.
- **Purpose:** Khader said steps would be taken to establish a separate MCC unit if the hospital records more than 100 deliveries a month, and that a meeting with MLA Mankal Vaidya and senior officials would be held soon to finalise plans; he also said the foundation stone for a Haj Bhavan in Mangaluru would be laid soon.

**Important points**
- Khader said steps would be taken to establish a separate MCC unit if the hospital records more than 100 deliveries a month, and that a meeting with MLA Mankal Vaidya and senior officials would be held soon to finalise plans.
- he also said the foundation stone for a Haj Bhavan in Mangaluru would be laid soon.
_Sources: News Trail Mangaluru p.3_

#### Puttur temple devotees seek probe into alleged irregularities

**In short:** Devotees and citizens of Puttur have petitioned the Karnataka Department of Religious Endowments seeking an inquiry into alleged administrative irregularities, rule violations and controversial developmental works at Sri Mahalingeshwara Temple, including demolition of the Naga Sannidhi, Ayyappa Swamy and Navagraha shrines without prior approval. The complaint also questions the dismantling of a Nataraja platform and a 3,000 sq ft building, the cutting of more than 15 coconut trees, filling of at least seven open wells, and a proposed 30-foot road along the northern side that complainants say could use around four acres of temple property.

#### Driver’s daughter gets first MBBS seat at Alva’s medical college

**In short:** Sneha, a student of Alva’s Pre-University College in Moodbidri and a resident of Kuntalpady in Karkala, is the first student admitted to the newly opened Alva’s Institute of Medical Sciences and Research Centre, with an MBBS seat under the government quota. She studied under Alva’s merit adoption scheme, scored 506 out of 720 in NEET, and earlier scored 590 marks in SSLC at JC’s High School; her father Manjunath has driven for Srinivas University, Mangaluru, for 12 years.

**In short:** The Karnataka government has appointed 2024-batch IPS officer Ranu Gupta, previously a District Probationary Training officer in Tumakuru, as Assistant Superintendent of Police of Bhatkal Sub-Division; she has assumed charge in Bhatkal following the government order.

#### Hindu organisations seek arrest of group that assaulted photocopy centre owners

**In short:** Amid heavy police presence, a large number of people joined the ‘Puttur Chalo Rally’ on Monday, jointly organised by the VHP, Bajrang Dal and Hindu Jagarana Vedike, demanding arrest of those who allegedly attacked copying-centre owners in Puttur on August 21. HJV member Shrikant Shetty Karkala said a complaint accusing the owners of assault was registered for attempt to murder, but no serious charges were filed against those who assaulted Sudharshan Pai and relatives Akshata Pai and Sadashiva Pai in the presence of police.

### Karnataka

#### HC quashes land acquisition for KHB project

**In short:** The High Court of Karnataka has quashed acquisition of 1,938 acres for the Karnataka Housing Board’s Suryanagar 4th phase residential layout, holding the land is part of areas proposed as eco-sensitive zone of Bannerghatta National Park and abuts elephant corridors; a Division Bench of Justice D.K. Singh and Justice H.

The High Court of Karnataka has quashed acquisition of 1,938 acres for the Karnataka Housing Board’s Suryanagar 4th phase residential layout, holding the land is part of areas proposed as eco-sensitive zone of Bannerghatta National Park and abuts elephant corridors; a Division Bench of Justice D.K. Singh and Justice H. Shanthi Bhushan passed the order on August 29, setting aside a single judge’s January 2025 verdict. The Bench said since the project falls within BNP’s proposed ESZ of 268.96 sq. km., the whole project is misconceived and would have irreversible ecological consequences; KHB began acquisition in 2013 across five Anekal taluk villages — Konasandra, Bommandahalli, Kadujakkanahalli, Indlavadi, and Bagganadoddi. MoEFCC proposed an ESZ of 268.96 sq. km in a 2016 draft, later reduced to 168.84 sq. km in a March 2020 final notification; the CEC’s January 2026 report on a plea by K.B. Belliappa recommended restoring 268.96 sq. km, while MoEFCC said it is studying that recommendation.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** HC quashes land acquisition for KHB project
- **Told:** The High Court of Karnataka has quashed acquisition of 1,938 acres for the Karnataka Housing Board’s Suryanagar 4th phase residential layout, holding the land is part of areas proposed as eco-sensitive zone of Bannerghatta National Park and abuts elephant corridors; a Division Bench of Justice D.K.
- **Purpose:** km, while MoEFCC said it is studying that recommendation.

**Important points**
- Shanthi Bhushan passed the order on August 29, setting aside a single judge’s January 2025 verdict.
- The Bench said since the project falls within BNP’s proposed ESZ of 268.96 sq.
- km., the whole project is misconceived and would have irreversible ecological consequences.
- KHB began acquisition in 2013 across five Anekal taluk villages — Konasandra, Bommandahalli, Kadujakkanahalli, Indlavadi, and Bagganadoddi.
- MoEFCC proposed an ESZ of 268.96 sq.
- km in a 2016 draft, later reduced to 168.84 sq.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.1; The Hindu Mangaluru p.5_

#### Rs 72 lakh for 30x40 site: BDA starts accepting applications for Karanth layout

**In short:** Chief Minister D K Shivakumar on Monday announced allotment of 2,815 sites to the general public in the 3,700-acre Dr K Shivaram Karanth Layout spread over 17 villages in northern Bengaluru, with a 30x40 sq ft site priced at Rs 72 lakh at Rs 6,000 per square foot. The Bangalore Development Authority (BDA) has started accepting applications through the Seva Sindhu portal; the layout comprises 24,714 sites in total, with 18,399 reserved for landowners who parted with land in 2020-21 and the project costing Rs 16,419.89 crore.

Chief Minister D K Shivakumar on Monday announced allotment of 2,815 sites to the general public in the 3,700-acre Dr K Shivaram Karanth Layout spread over 17 villages in northern Bengaluru, with a 30x40 sq ft site priced at Rs 72 lakh at Rs 6,000 per square foot. The Bangalore Development Authority (BDA) has started accepting applications through the Seva Sindhu portal; the layout comprises 24,714 sites in total, with 18,399 reserved for landowners who parted with land in 2020-21 and the project costing Rs 16,419.89 crore. Shivakumar hinted it could be the BDA's last layout given land acquisition difficulties and said site prices would rise further when the Bengaluru Business Corridor comes up.

#### Independent judiciary vital to protect human rights: ICJ judge

**In short:** An independent and impartial judiciary, coupled with collective global responsibility, is essential to protecting human rights and addressing emerging challenges, International Court of Justice (ICJ) Judge Leonardo Nemer Caldeira Brant said on Monday delivering a special lecture at CMR University's School of Legal Studies in Bengaluru. Judge Brant said the ICJ plays a crucial role in upholding international law and that human rights had evolved into a shared global responsibility requiring independent judicial institutions.

An independent and impartial judiciary, coupled with collective global responsibility, is essential to protecting human rights and addressing emerging challenges, International Court of Justice (ICJ) Judge Leonardo Nemer Caldeira Brant said on Monday delivering a special lecture at CMR University's School of Legal Studies in Bengaluru. Judge Brant said the ICJ plays a crucial role in upholding international law and that human rights had evolved into a shared global responsibility requiring independent judicial institutions. He underlined the growing importance of international legal mechanisms in dealing with challenges such as climate change and environmental protection, and urged civil society to remain actively engaged in protecting fundamental rights.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Independent judiciary vital to protect human rights: ICJ judge
- **Told:** An independent and impartial judiciary, coupled with collective global responsibility, is essential to protecting human rights and addressing emerging challenges, International Court of Justice (ICJ) Judge Leonardo Nemer Caldeira Brant said on Monday delivering a special lecture at CMR University's School of Legal Studies in Bengaluru.
- **Purpose:** He underlined the growing importance of international legal mechanisms in dealing with challenges such as climate change and environmental protection, and urged civil society to remain actively engaged in protecting fundamental rights.

**Important points**
- He underlined the growing importance of international legal mechanisms in dealing with challenges such as climate change and environmental protection, and urged civil society to remain actively engaged in protecting fundamental rights.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.5_

#### Nimhans unveils revamped creche for faculty children

**In short:** The National Institute of Mental Health and Neuro Sciences (Nimhans) on Monday inaugurated its renovated creche for faculty members' children, designed around experiential learning and a child-first approach. The upgraded facility features sensory play areas with varied surfaces, designated activity zones, ample space for movement and better natural lighting; Dr Eesha Sharma, Associate Professor, Department of Child and Adolescent Psychiatry, said the nearly 40-year-old creche was redesigned for children's overall, mental and emotional development.

The National Institute of Mental Health and Neuro Sciences (Nimhans) on Monday inaugurated its renovated creche for faculty members' children, designed around experiential learning and a child-first approach. The upgraded facility features sensory play areas with varied surfaces, designated activity zones, ample space for movement and better natural lighting; Dr Eesha Sharma, Associate Professor, Department of Child and Adolescent Psychiatry, said the nearly 40-year-old creche was redesigned for children's overall, mental and emotional development. Architect Bhavana Kumar undertook the renovation as a porous open space allowing caretakers to monitor children from any corner; about 25% of attending children are aged seven to eight.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Nimhans unveils revamped creche for faculty children

#### Transport minister makes surprise visits to KSRTC, BMTC stations in Bengaluru

**In short:** Transport Minister Byrathi Suresh on Monday made surprise visits to the KSRTC and BMTC bus stations at Majestic, inspecting passenger facilities and interacting with commuters about cleanliness and basic amenities. He expressed displeasure after finding no doctor available at the clinic at the BMTC station and directed the BMTC managing director to arrange one, also instructing officials to ensure first-aid and essential medical facilities on the premises.

Transport Minister Byrathi Suresh on Monday made surprise visits to the KSRTC and BMTC bus stations at Majestic, inspecting passenger facilities and interacting with commuters about cleanliness and basic amenities. He expressed displeasure after finding no doctor available at the clinic at the BMTC station and directed the BMTC managing director to arrange one, also instructing officials to ensure first-aid and essential medical facilities on the premises. The minister inspected shops at both stations, directed officials to prevent overcharging, and visited the BMTC student pass counter to address delays in free bus pass distribution.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Transport minister makes surprise visits to KSRTC, BMTC stations in Bengaluru

#### 18 years after notification, Authority finally allots sites to land-losers

**In short:** For landowners who waited nearly two decades since the Dr Shivaram Karanth Layout was first notified in 2008, the BDA's commencement of site allotment on Monday marks a major milestone after prolonged litigation and Supreme Court intervention in 2018. A committee headed by Justice A V Chandrashekar, formed in December 2020, coordinated land acquisition and layout formation, with civil works split among nine construction firms from December 2022; landowners such as M Devaraj of Kalathammanahalli received six developed sites for about 32 guntas surrendered.

For landowners who waited nearly two decades since the Dr Shivaram Karanth Layout was first notified in 2008, the BDA's commencement of site allotment on Monday marks a major milestone after prolonged litigation and Supreme Court intervention in 2018. A committee headed by Justice A V Chandrashekar, formed in December 2020, coordinated land acquisition and layout formation, with civil works split among nine construction firms from December 2022; landowners such as M Devaraj of Kalathammanahalli received six developed sites for about 32 guntas surrendered. The BDA invested around Rs 16,000 crore in the layout and did not acknowledge the SC-monitored committee's role at Monday's allotment ceremony.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** 18 years after notification, Authority finally allots sites to land-losers
- **Told:** For landowners who waited nearly two decades since the Dr Shivaram Karanth Layout was first notified in 2008, the BDA's commencement of site allotment on Monday marks a major milestone after prolonged litigation and Supreme Court intervention in 2018.
- **Purpose:** The BDA invested around Rs 16,000 crore in the layout and did not acknowledge the SC-monitored committee's role at Monday's allotment ceremony.

**Important points**
- The BDA invested around Rs 16,000 crore in the layout and did not acknowledge the SC-monitored committee's role at Monday's allotment ceremony.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.5_

#### Threats against event organisers won't be tolerated: Priyank

**In short:** Home Minister Priyank Kharge on Monday said threats on social media against people organising events would not be tolerated, responding to questions about the Karnataka High Court pulling up Whitefield police over alleged illegal arrests and referring to the station as "Darkfield." Kharge said there had been a communication gap and that the person detained was a repeat offender not connected to comedian Kunal Kamra; Whitefield police had registered a suo motu FIR after Mohan Gowda of the Hindu Rashtra Samanvaya Samiti posted on social media that cancellation of an event was a victory for Hindu unity. In a separate interaction with over 800 police officers, Kharge said the government would stand by honest policemen but not protect those acting against the law.

#### 6-member committees for reverification of guarantee beneficiaries

**In short:** The Karnataka government has constituted six-member committees at panchayat and ward levels for reverification of beneficiaries of the five flagship guarantee schemes, with each committee comprising two women, one SC/ST, one general category, one minority and one OBC member. Guarantee Implementation Authority Chairman H M Revanna said reverification is expected to involve filling forms via an app with phone numbers, photos and addresses; Chief Minister D K Shivakumar held a meeting on Monday where major decisions on reverification were taken.

The Karnataka government has constituted six-member committees at panchayat and ward levels for reverification of beneficiaries of the five flagship guarantee schemes, with each committee comprising two women, one SC/ST, one general category, one minority and one OBC member. Guarantee Implementation Authority Chairman H M Revanna said reverification is expected to involve filling forms via an app with phone numbers, photos and addresses; Chief Minister D K Shivakumar held a meeting on Monday where major decisions on reverification were taken. The government is planning a convention to mark three years of the guarantee schemes, with total expenditure on all five guarantees at Rs 1.48 lakh crore.

**What this is**

#### Gruha Jyothi beneficiary survey deadline extended

**In short:** The Karnataka government has extended until September 30 the deadline for the ongoing door-to-door survey of beneficiaries under the Gruha Jyothi scheme, which provides up to 200 units of free electricity to households as one of the Congress government's five guarantee schemes. The verification survey, launched in July 2023, began on July 1 with an initial completion deadline of August 31; BESCOM said 46,70,588 of 68,86,860 beneficiaries across eight districts under its jurisdiction, nearly 68 per cent, had been covered as of August 31.

The Karnataka government has extended until September 30 the deadline for the ongoing door-to-door survey of beneficiaries under the Gruha Jyothi scheme, which provides up to 200 units of free electricity to households as one of the Congress government's five guarantee schemes. #### Food safety crackdown on 16 sweet manufacturing units in state; major violations found

**In short:** Food Safety and Drug Administration officials on Monday conducted a special inspection drive at sweet manufacturing units in Bengaluru Urban, Bengaluru Rural, Greater Bengaluru Authority, Mysuru, Dakshina Kannada, Belagavi, Kalaburagi and Hubballi-Dharwad, collecting samples of sweets, cooking oil, khova, Mysore Pak and other products for laboratory examination. Major violations included storage of expired food products, labelling not in accordance with FSSAI regulations, use of unauthorised artificial colours and failure to mention mandatory label declarations; units inspected included Asha Sweets and Bikaner Sweets in Bengaluru, Swagath Sweets in Mangaluru and World of Mysore Pak in Mysuru.

Food Safety and Drug Administration officials on Monday conducted a special inspection drive at sweet manufacturing units in Bengaluru Urban, Bengaluru Rural, Greater Bengaluru Authority, Mysuru, Dakshina Kannada, Belagavi, Kalaburagi and Hubballi-Dharwad, collecting samples of sweets, cooking oil, khova, Mysore Pak and other products for laboratory examination. Major violations included storage of expired food products, labelling not in accordance with FSSAI regulations, use of unauthorised artificial colours and failure to mention mandatory label declarations; units inspected included Asha Sweets and Bikaner Sweets in Bengaluru, Swagath Sweets in Mangaluru and World of Mysore Pak in Mysuru. Officials also found incorrect labelling, improper storage, unhygienic manufacturing conditions and other violations during the drive.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Food safety crackdown on 16 sweet manufacturing units in state; major violations found
- **Told:** Food Safety and Drug Administration officials on Monday conducted a special inspection drive at sweet manufacturing units in Bengaluru Urban, Bengaluru Rural, Greater Bengaluru Authority, Mysuru, Dakshina Kannada, Belagavi, Kalaburagi and Hubballi-Dharwad, collecting samples of sweets, cooking oil, khova, Mysore Pak and other products for laboratory examination.
- **Purpose:** Officials also found incorrect labelling, improper storage, unhygienic manufacturing conditions and other violations during the drive.

**Important points**
- Officials also found incorrect labelling, improper storage, unhygienic manufacturing conditions and other violations during the drive.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.5; The Hindu Mangaluru p.8_

#### Prajaseva campaign to start Sept 5

**In short:** Chief Minister D K Shivakumar's Prajaseva citizen connect campaign will start on September 5 from Kudligi hobli in Vijayanagar district, Chief Secretary Shalini Rajneesh said in a statement on Monday. The Prajaseva Abhiyana will be an integrated system for directly hearing citizens' grievances and providing redress on the spot or within a specified timeframe; meetings will be held on the first and third Saturdays at taluk headquarters under district in-charge ministers and on the second and fourth Saturdays at taluk/hobli level under local MLAs.

Chief Minister D K Shivakumar's Prajaseva citizen connect campaign will start on September 5 from Kudligi hobli in Vijayanagar district, Chief Secretary Shalini Rajneesh said in a statement on Monday. The Prajaseva Abhiyana will be an integrated system for directly hearing citizens' grievances and providing redress on the spot or within a specified timeframe; meetings will be held on the first and third Saturdays at taluk headquarters under district in-charge ministers and on the second and fourth Saturdays at taluk/hobli level under local MLAs. The government has developed the Integrated Public Grievance Redressal System software, and citizens can register grievances on helpline 1902 or through ipgrs.karnataka.gov.in.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Prajaseva campaign to start Sept 5
- **Told:** Chief Minister D K Shivakumar's Prajaseva citizen connect campaign will start on September 5 from Kudligi hobli in Vijayanagar district, Chief Secretary Shalini Rajneesh said in a statement on Monday.
- **Purpose:** The government has developed the Integrated Public Grievance Redressal System software, and citizens can register grievances on helpline 1902 or through ipgrs.karnataka.gov.in.

#### Amid KPSC probe, govt drafts rules for stringent exam law

**In short:** Coinciding with the investigation into alleged scams at the Karnataka Public Service Commission (KPSC), the state government has published draft rules to operationalise the Karnataka Public Examination (Measures for Prevention of Corruption and Unfair Means in Recruitment) Act, 2023, notified in September 2024. Those found guilty of unfair means face imprisonment of five to 12 years and fines from Rs 10 lakh to Rs 10 crore; draft rules published by the Home Department on August 24 propose investigations into offences including question paper leaks be completed within three months.

Coinciding with the investigation into alleged scams at the Karnataka Public Service Commission (KPSC), the state government has published draft rules to operationalise the Karnataka Public Examination (Measures for Prevention of Corruption and Unfair Means in Recruitment) Act, 2023, notified in September 2024. Those found guilty of unfair means face imprisonment of five to 12 years and fines from Rs 10 lakh to Rs 10 crore; draft rules published by the Home Department on August 24 propose investigations into offences including question paper leaks be completed within three months. The rules permit biometric verification, QR code tracking, GPS tracking, CCTV surveillance and AI-based anomaly detection, and bar candidates from carrying mobile phones, smart watches and other prohibited devices unless specifically permitted.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Amid KPSC probe, govt drafts rules for stringent exam law
- **Told:** Coinciding with the investigation into alleged scams at the Karnataka Public Service Commission (KPSC), the state government has published draft rules to operationalise the Karnataka Public Examination (Measures for Prevention of Corruption and Unfair Means in Recruitment) Act, 2023, notified in September 2024.
- **Purpose:** The rules permit biometric verification, QR code tracking, GPS tracking, CCTV surveillance and AI-based anomaly detection, and bar candidates from carrying mobile phones, smart watches and other prohibited devices unless specifically permitted.

**Important points**
- The rules permit biometric verification.
- QR code tracking.
- GPS tracking.
- CCTV surveillance and AI-based anomaly detection, and bar candidates from carrying mobile phones, smart watches and other prohibited devices unless specifically permitted.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.6_

#### KPSC scam: CID arrests middleman who made crores

**In short:** The Criminal Investigation Department has arrested Basavaraj Kannale, a Bhalki-based alleged middleman, in connection with the KPSC veterinary officers recruitment scam, opening a new line of investigation into the suspected network behind alleged recruitment irregularities. Investigators are examining allegations that he used purported political contacts and photographs with leaders of both the BJP and Congress to convince aspirants he had influence over recruitment, and are probing his financial dealings, communications and links with officials including an alleged connection with IAS officer Gyanendra Kumar.

The Criminal Investigation Department has arrested Basavaraj Kannale, a Bhalki-based alleged middleman, in connection with the KPSC veterinary officers recruitment scam, opening a new line of investigation into the suspected network behind alleged recruitment irregularities. Investigators are examining allegations that he used purported political contacts and photographs with leaders of both the BJP and Congress to convince aspirants he had influence over recruitment, and are probing his financial dealings, communications and links with officials including an alleged connection with IAS officer Gyanendra Kumar. He is allegedly the owner of property in Bengaluru and Bhalki and is also alleged to have purchased land worth crores of rupees in his native village.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** KPSC scam: CID arrests middleman who made crores
- **Told:** The Criminal Investigation Department has arrested Basavaraj Kannale, a Bhalki-based alleged middleman, in connection with the KPSC veterinary officers recruitment scam, opening a new line of investigation into the suspected network behind alleged recruitment irregularities.
- **Purpose:** He is allegedly the owner of property in Bengaluru and Bhalki and is also alleged to have purchased land worth crores of rupees in his native village.

**Important points**
- He is allegedly the owner of property in Bengaluru and Bhalki and is also alleged to have purchased land worth crores of rupees in his native village.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.6; News Trail Mangaluru p.2; The Hindu Mangaluru p.5_

#### ED issues lookout notice against Commission member Shanta Hosamani

**In short:** The Enforcement Directorate (ED), separately probing alleged KPSC irregularities, has issued a lookout notice at airports against KPSC member Shanta Hosamani, who has been untraceable since ED raids on August 18 and has failed to respond to ED notices. Home Minister Priyank Kharge said the ED's swift action raised doubts and that it appeared to be protecting BJP leaders, noting photographs of alleged mastermind Basavaraj Kannale with state BJP leaders including Karnataka BJP president B Y Vijayendra and MLA C N Ashwath Narayan had gone viral.

The Enforcement Directorate (ED), separately probing alleged KPSC irregularities, has issued a lookout notice at airports against KPSC member Shanta Hosamani, who has been untraceable since ED raids on August 18 and has failed to respond to ED notices. KPSC, which conducts competitive exams for Karnataka government jobs, is facing scrutiny over alleged irregularities in the veterinary officers' recruitment exam.

#### CM says K'swamy targeting BJP, not Cong on KPSC scam

**In short:** Karnataka Chief Minister D K Shivakumar said JD(S) leader H D Kumaraswamy was targeting the BJP, not the Congress, over the alleged KPSC scam and was free to make whatever allegations he wanted. Shivakumar said he would not interfere in the investigation including arrests of an IAS officer, that the BJP was free to undertake its padayatra from Lingasugur to Ballari from Tuesday, and that a proposed land guarantee initiative would benefit all whose property is acquired for government purposes across the state, not only Bengaluru.

#### Energy dept to accelerate installation of solar rooftop systems on govt bldgs

**In short:** The state government will accelerate installation of Solar Rooftop Photovoltaic (SRTPV) systems on government buildings under the PM Surya Ghar Muft Bijli Yojana, the energy department said on Monday, noting that though close to 59,000 government buildings have potential for SRTPV, only 2,624 have systems installed. Energy Minister K J George said priority will be given to Deputy Commissioners' offices and other government buildings with adequate rooftop space; under the RESCO model, developers bear the entire project cost and operation and maintenance, while departments can also opt for a CAPEX model with EPC developers selected through bidding.

The state government will accelerate installation of Solar Rooftop Photovoltaic (SRTPV) systems on government buildings under the PM Surya Ghar Muft Bijli Yojana, the energy department said on Monday, noting that though close to 59,000 government buildings have potential for SRTPV, only 2,624 have systems installed. Energy Minister K J George said priority will be given to Deputy Commissioners' offices and other government buildings with adequate rooftop space; under the RESCO model, developers bear the entire project cost and operation and maintenance, while departments can also opt for a CAPEX model with EPC developers selected through bidding. Bescom and other ESCOMs will extend support for grid connectivity, metering and regulatory compliance.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Energy dept to accelerate installation of solar rooftop systems on govt bldgs
- **Told:** The state government will accelerate installation of Solar Rooftop Photovoltaic (SRTPV) systems on government buildings under the PM Surya Ghar Muft Bijli Yojana, the energy department said on Monday, noting that though close to 59,000 government buildings have potential for SRTPV, only 2,624 have systems installed.
- **Purpose:** Bescom and other ESCOMs will extend support for grid connectivity, metering and regulatory compliance.

**Important points**
- Bescom and other ESCOMs will extend support for grid connectivity, metering and regulatory compliance.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.6; News Trail Mangaluru p.5_

#### Wild jumbo electrocuted

**In short:** A wild female elephant aged around 35 years died of electrocution at Kogilamane village in Belur taluk of Hassan district on Monday after coming in contact with a live wire under an electricity pole. A herd of wild elephants had strayed into the village in search of food when the jumbo was electrocuted on the spot; forest department officials visited the spot and veterinarian Dr Vinayaka of Bhadra Tiger Reserve conducted the post-mortem.

#### Has HDK taken contract from RSS to target me?: Priyank

**In short:** Home Minister Priyank Kharge on Monday asked whether Union Minister H D Kumaraswamy had taken a contract from the RSS to target him, continuing to refute Kumaraswamy's allegations over KPSC irregularities in the appointment of 24 Assistant Executive Engineers to the RDPR Department. Priyank noted KPSC itself released the AEE list, formed committees to look into allegations and wrote on February 22, 2024 asking RDPR to withhold the final selection list, and said the RDPR appointment order was conditional pending the High Court's final decision.

Home Minister Priyank Kharge on Monday asked whether Union Minister H D Kumaraswamy had taken a contract from the RSS to target him, continuing to refute Kumaraswamy's allegations over KPSC irregularities in the appointment of 24 Assistant Executive Engineers to the RDPR Department. He said if his role in KPSC matters was established either directly or indirectly, he was ready to take political retirement.

#### Kumaraswamy questions Rahul's silence over KPSC recruitment scam

**In short:** Union Steel Minister H D Kumaraswamy on Monday questioned the silence of Congress leader and Leader of Opposition in the Lok Sabha Rahul Gandhi over the KPSC recruitment scam, urging him to visit Bengaluru to safeguard the interests of thousands of job aspirants. Kumaraswamy noted Gandhi has been vocal on the NEET examination issue but maintained stoic silence on Karnataka job recruitment irregularities, and alleged the Congress-ruled state government was working to protect those involved.

Union Steel Minister H D Kumaraswamy on Monday questioned the silence of Congress leader and Leader of Opposition in the Lok Sabha Rahul Gandhi over the KPSC recruitment scam, urging him to visit Bengaluru to safeguard the interests of thousands of job aspirants. He held former RDPR Minister Priyank Kharge responsible for irregularities in Assistant Executive Engineer recruitment and said the state ordered a CID probe only after the ED began its investigation.

#### Nursing colleges accused of charging excess fees from students

**In short:** Following the second round of seat allotment for professional courses, the Karnataka Examinations' Authority (KEA) has received complaints that some nursing colleges are charging excess fees beyond the prescribed Rs 10,000 for government quota, Rs 1 lakh for Karnataka management quota and Rs 1.4 lakh for non-Karnataka management quota seats. Some colleges are allegedly demanding an additional Rs 50,000 from government quota students; the Admission Overseeing Committee at Rajiv Gandhi University of Health Sciences (RGUHS) and KEA have received numerous complaints.

Following the second round of seat allotment for professional courses, the Karnataka Examinations' Authority (KEA) has received complaints that some nursing colleges are charging excess fees beyond the prescribed Rs 10,000 for government quota, Rs 1 lakh for Karnataka management quota and Rs 1.4 lakh for non-Karnataka management quota seats. Some colleges are allegedly demanding an additional Rs 50,000 from government quota students; the Admission Overseeing Committee at Rajiv Gandhi University of Health Sciences (RGUHS) and KEA have received numerous complaints. RGUHS warned colleges failing to adhere to the fee structure risk losing affiliation.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Nursing colleges accused of charging excess fees from students
- **Told:** Following the second round of seat allotment for professional courses, the Karnataka Examinations' Authority (KEA) has received complaints that some nursing colleges are charging excess fees beyond the prescribed Rs 10,000 for government quota, Rs 1 lakh for Karnataka management quota and Rs 1.4 lakh for non-Karnataka management quota seats.
- **Purpose:** RGUHS warned colleges failing to adhere to the fee structure risk losing affiliation.

**Important points**
- RGUHS warned colleges failing to adhere to the fee structure risk losing affiliation.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.6_

#### FIR against medical officer for making patients clean hospital cesspit

**In short:** The medical officer of Savanur Taluk Hospital in Haveri district, Dr S Y Hiregowdar, and nursing supervisor Vaneetha were named in an FIR on Monday after two patients were tasked with cleaning the hospital cesspit on August 28. Haveri Deputy Commissioner Vijay Mahantesh Danammanavar directed the tahsildar to register the FIR at Savanur police station after Social Welfare Department officials visited the hospital to ascertain facts; both have been summoned for questioning and the District Health Officer is learnt to have recommended suspending them.

#### Develop dashboard-based online system to lodge plaints: Khandre

**In short:** Rural Development and Panchayat Raj (RDPR) Minister Eshwar Khandre has directed officials to develop a dashboard-based online system for registering and resolving complaints related to gram panchayat services including e-Swathu, property tax, lake encroachments, Jal Jeevan Mission, sanitation and rural roads. Khandre said the RDPR department's direct contact with nearly half the state's population made prompt grievance redress important, and directed his secretary to develop a web portal and application allowing rural residents to register complaints with videos and photographs.

Rural Development and Panchayat Raj (RDPR) Minister Eshwar Khandre has directed officials to develop a dashboard-based online system for registering and resolving complaints related to gram panchayat services including e-Swathu, property tax, lake encroachments, Jal Jeevan Mission, sanitation and rural roads. Khandre said the RDPR department's direct contact with nearly half the state's population made prompt grievance redress important, and directed his secretary to develop a web portal and application allowing rural residents to register complaints with videos and photographs. The system should let complainants track status, access the responsible officer's contact details and ensure resolution within a stipulated timeframe.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Develop dashboard-based online system to lodge plaints: Khandre
- **Told:** Rural Development and Panchayat Raj (RDPR) Minister Eshwar Khandre has directed officials to develop a dashboard-based online system for registering and resolving complaints related to gram panchayat services including e-Swathu, property tax, lake encroachments, Jal Jeevan Mission, sanitation and rural roads.
- **Purpose:** The system should let complainants track status, access the responsible officer's contact details and ensure resolution within a stipulated timeframe.

_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.6_

#### Rayareddi should apologise to Hindus for his remarks on religion: Kageri

**In short:** Uttara Kannada BJP MP Vishweshwar Hegde Kageri on Monday demanded Higher Education Minister Basavaraj Rayareddi tender an apology to Hindus over his "anti-Hindu" remarks, saying Rayareddi's communal appeasement had demeaned Sanatana Dharma and the faith centres of millions of Hindus. Kageri warned of a statewide agitation if the government fails to initiate strict action against the minister.

Uttara Kannada BJP MP Vishweshwar Hegde Kageri on Monday demanded Higher Education Minister Basavaraj Rayareddi tender an apology to Hindus over his "anti-Hindu" remarks, saying Rayareddi's communal appeasement had demeaned Sanatana Dharma and the faith centres of millions of Hindus. Kageri warned of a statewide agitation if the government fails to initiate strict action against the minister. Rayareddi clarified on Monday in Koppal that he had only spoken about good qualities in Islam and never said other religions were inferior, after sparking controversy on Sunday in Kukanur by describing Islam as the world's best religion and expressing desire to perform Hajj.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Rayareddi should apologise to Hindus for his remarks on religion: Kageri

- **Purpose:** Rayareddi clarified on Monday in Koppal that he had only spoken about good qualities in Islam and never said other religions were inferior, after sparking controversy on Sunday in Kukanur by describing Islam as the world's best religion and expressing desire to perform Hajj.

#### 20 lakh hectares unsown as sowing closes for kharif crops

**In short:** As the sowing window for most kharif crops draws to a close, agriculture department data as of August 28 shows 20 lakh hectares of the 84.1 lakh hectare kharif target remained unsown, with cereals alone accounting for 15 lakh uncultivated hectares amid deficit rainfall and adverse weather. Paddy has been sown on only 4.87 lakh hectares against a 10 lakh target, ragi on 4.62 against 8.2 lakh and maize on 13 against 17.3 lakh; Agriculture Minister P M Narendraswamy said good sowing need not mean a good crop and that the department has received over 37 lakh crop insurance applications, an all-time high.

As the sowing window for most kharif crops draws to a close, agriculture department data as of August 28 shows 20 lakh hectares of the 84.1 lakh hectare kharif target remained unsown, with cereals alone accounting for 15 lakh uncultivated hectares amid deficit rainfall and adverse weather. Paddy has been sown on only 4.87 lakh hectares against a 10 lakh target, ragi on 4.62 against 8.2 lakh and maize on 13 against 17.3 lakh; Agriculture Minister P M Narendraswamy said good sowing need not mean a good crop and that the department has received over 37 lakh crop insurance applications, an all-time high. Bagalkot is the only district to have covered 100% of its sowing target, with five North Karnataka districts crossing 90% cultivation.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** 20 lakh hectares unsown as sowing closes for kharif crops
- **Told:** As the sowing window for most kharif crops draws to a close, agriculture department data as of August 28 shows 20 lakh hectares of the 84.1 lakh hectare kharif target remained unsown, with cereals alone accounting for 15 lakh uncultivated hectares amid deficit rainfall and adverse weather.
- **Purpose:** Bagalkot is the only district to have covered 100% of its sowing target, with five North Karnataka districts crossing 90% cultivation.

**Important points**
- Bagalkot is the only district to have covered 100% of its sowing target, with five North Karnataka districts crossing 90% cultivation.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.7_

#### BJP to launch 'Ballari Chalo' against Cong 'misrule' today

**In short:** The BJP has prepared for its 175-km padayatra beginning from Lingasugur in Raichur district on Tuesday (September 1), passing through Maski, Sindhanur, Karatagi, Gangavati, Kampli and Kudatini before reaching Ballari in Sandur constituency over 10 days. Senior leaders including Union Education Minister Pralhad Joshi, BJP state president B Y Vijayendra, LoP R Ashoka and Deputy LoP Arvind Bellad are expected to participate; around 30,000 people are expected at the launch on a 4-acre stage on the Lingasugur bypass and about 700 police personnel have been deputed.

The BJP has prepared for its 175-km padayatra beginning from Lingasugur in Raichur district on Tuesday (September 1), passing through Maski, Sindhanur, Karatagi, Gangavati, Kampli and Kudatini before reaching Ballari in Sandur constituency over 10 days. Senior leaders including Union Education Minister Pralhad Joshi, BJP state president B Y Vijayendra, LoP R Ashoka and Deputy LoP Arvind Bellad are expected to participate; around 30,000 people are expected at the launch on a 4-acre stage on the Lingasugur bypass and about 700 police personnel have been deputed. The party said the march is to pressure the government over the Valmiki Corporation and KPSC scams and to declare all taluks drought-affected.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** BJP to launch 'Ballari Chalo' against Cong 'misrule' today
- **Told:** The BJP has prepared for its 175-km padayatra beginning from Lingasugur in Raichur district on Tuesday (September 1), passing through Maski, Sindhanur, Karatagi, Gangavati, Kampli and Kudatini before reaching Ballari in Sandur constituency over 10 days.
- **Purpose:** The party said the march is to pressure the government over the Valmiki Corporation and KPSC scams and to declare all taluks drought-affected.

**Important points**
- The party said the march is to pressure the government over the Valmiki Corporation and KPSC scams and to declare all taluks drought-affected.

#### Nepal flood survivors recount narrow escape

**In short:** Karnataka tourists caught in Nepal's devastating flash floods recounted a desperate fight for survival, with survivors describing a deafening roar, torrents of water and debris leaving little time to react. Basavaraj Hosamani (52) of Haveri district, part of a group from Rattihalli, said a traffic jam proved their saviour on August 26 when they learned the magnitude of devastation along the Trishuli River route they had photographed just 24 hours earlier; the group of 54 returned to Bengaluru on August 29 by flight after a 13-hour journey to the Sonauli border instead of the usual four hours due to destroyed roads and bridges.

#### 13 from K'taka still untraced after floods: CM

**In short:** Karnataka Chief Minister D K Shivakumar said on Monday that about 13 people from the state were yet to be traced following flash floods along the Nepal-Tibet border and adjoining areas last week. The state government is in contact with the Ministry of External Affairs and the central government, with representatives sent to establish contact; Shivakumar said one group has not been traced while the rest have been found.

#### Two boys assaulted with pipe over unpaid Boost packet

**In short:** The owner of Manikantha Super Bazaar in Malaghaana village of Almel taluk, Vijayapura district, was arrested by Almel police for allegedly assaulting two boys with a pipe over an accusation that they had not paid for a packet of Boost picked up from the store. The boys, who sustained serious injuries, were admitted to Sindagi government hospital for treatment; a video purportedly showing the supermarket owner assaulting the boys with a pipe has circulated on social media.

**In short:** The Board of Directors of JSW MG Motor India on Monday appointed Parth Jindal as Chairman of the company, with immediate effect. #### ITC Infotech to buy 22.1% stake in Happiest Minds for Rs 1,330 cr

**In short:** Bengaluru-based Happiest Minds will merge with ITC Infotech India Limited, with the latter acquiring an aggregate 22.1% minority stake from promoter Ashok Soota and promoter entities across two tranches for a total consideration of Rs 1,330 crore. The combined entity will be an AI-first global technology services enterprise targeting $1 billion of annual revenue by FY28, with over 19,000 employees and ITC Limited holding a 73.4% stake post-merger under a share swap of 25 ITC Infotech shares for every 81 Happiest Minds shares.

#### Why your onion bhajjis might get costlier

**In short:** Onion prices have surged in Bengaluru amid a rainfall deficit in Karnataka, with retail prices rising from around Rs 15–20 per kg to Rs 40–50 per kg. With festivals around the corner, vendors expect prices to rise further, and one vendor told News Trail that the price of half a quintal (50 kg) had increased significantly.

#### Shikshagraha partners with Teach for India

**In short:** Shikshagraha on Monday announced a partnership with Teach For India, combining the latter's 17 years of experience in nurturing teachers, young leaders and alumni with a movement that has already mobilised more than 3.9 lakh leaders across 2.1 lakh public schools. Shikshagraha, a people's movement working to strengthen one million public schools across India, said Teach For India brings classroom leadership experience while Shikshagraha contributes a network of more than 40 civil society organisations; it cited more than 15.6 lakh micro-improvements across public schools led by over 3.9 lakh leaders reaching more than 2.1 lakh schools.

**In short:** Two US citizens originally from Tumakuru—Rangusudheer, 53, and Keerthi Acharya, 50, residents of Nrupatunga Layout—are among those reported missing after devastating floods in Nepal, leaving family members in Karnataka deeply worried. Both software engineers had travelled to Nepal from the US on August 22 as part of a 10-member group; Rangusudheer last spoke to a relative at around 8.29am on August 26, after which the family lost contact with both.

#### CWMA water-release order being enforced: Karnataka

**In short:** Karnataka told the Supreme Court it was complying with the Cauvery Water Management Authority direction to ensure 9,000 cusecs of Cauvery water to Tamil Nadu, after CWMA on August 25 upheld the CWRC recommendation of 9,000 cusecs for 15 days. Senior advocate Shyam Divan said 9,888 cusecs were released on August 30 and flow had crossed 11,000 cusecs by Monday morning, while Tamil Nadu sought 17.604 tmc ft as the balance due as of August 27 under the pro-rata formula.

Karnataka told the Supreme Court it was complying with the Cauvery Water Management Authority direction to ensure 9,000 cusecs of Cauvery water to Tamil Nadu, after CWMA on August 25 upheld the CWRC recommendation of 9,000 cusecs for 15 days. Senior advocate Shyam Divan said 9,888 cusecs were released on August 30 and flow had crossed 11,000 cusecs by Monday morning, while Tamil Nadu sought 17.604 tmc ft as the balance due as of August 27 under the pro-rata formula. The court posted further hearing for September 15.

#### BJP plans 10-day padayatra to Ballari over govt's 'failures'

**In short:** The Karnataka BJP will launch a 10-day 'Ballari Chalo' foot march from Lingasugur in Raichur to Ballari on Tuesday to protest alleged failures, anti-people policies and corruption of the Congress government, covering approximately 185 km through multiple constituencies. Key reasons include demands over Minister B Nagendra, who resigned on Friday amid Valmiki corporation embezzlement allegations, though differences have emerged between BJP and JD(S) alliance partners over holding the march after his resignation.

#### Three of family run over by train

**In short:** A woman and her two granddaughters — Irfana and granddaughters Rukhiya and Raziya, from Hindupur in Andhra Pradesh — were killed on the spot after being run over by an express train between Doddaballapur and Muttur on Monday morning. The girls had come to their grandmother’s house from Hindupur for the holidays; the family was walking towards Doddaballapur railway station from Muttur when they stepped on the railway track where two tracks meet at a curve and allegedly failed to notice the approaching train.

#### Do not charge extra fees, RGUHS warns nursing and AHS colleges

**In short:** RGUHS directed all affiliated Nursing, Allied Health Sciences and Physiotherapy colleges not to collect any amount beyond government-prescribed fees, warning of strict action for violations. Nursing fees should not exceed ₹10,000 per annum for government quota seats, ₹1 lakh for management quota for Karnataka candidates and ₹1.40 lakh for non-Karnataka candidates; complaints allege colleges charging up to ₹50,000 in excess for government quota seats.

Admissions must be through KEA counselling.

#### KPSC ‘scam’: Priyank offers to quit politics if there is iota of evidence against him

**In short:** Home Minister Priyank Kharge offered to resign if even an iota of evidence showed his involvement in the alleged KPSC AEE recruitment scam under the Rural Drinking Water Supply Scheme, and challenged Union Minister H.D. Kumaraswamy to retire if BJP or JD(S) involvement was found.

Home Minister Priyank Kharge offered to resign if even an iota of evidence showed his involvement in the alleged KPSC AEE recruitment scam under the Rural Drinking Water Supply Scheme, and challenged Union Minister H.D. Kumaraswamy to retire if BJP or JD(S) involvement was found. Kharge said two FIRs had already been registered, OMR sheets were in KPSC custody, a five-member committee submitted a report a day after RDPR issued conditional appointment orders, and KPSC shared findings only in February this year, blaming the examination wing not the government. Kumaraswamy, speaking in Delhi, said he wanted a transparent probe not Kharge’s retirement and alleged candidates were kept at resorts, question papers provided and OMR sheets manipulated.

- **Concept:** KPSC ‘scam’: Priyank offers to quit politics if there is iota of evidence against him
- **Told:** Home Minister Priyank Kharge offered to resign if even an iota of evidence showed his involvement in the alleged KPSC AEE recruitment scam under the Rural Drinking Water Supply Scheme, and challenged Union Minister H.D.
- **Purpose:** Kumaraswamy, speaking in Delhi, said he wanted a transparent probe not Kharge’s retirement and alleged candidates were kept at resorts, question papers provided and OMR sheets manipulated.

#### Prajaseva Abhiyan to be launched on Sept. 5

**In short:** Chief Minister D.K. Shivakumar’s Prajaseva Abhiyan will launch at Kudligi in Vijayanagara district on September 5, with taluk and hobli meetings every Saturday — the district-in-charge Minister chairing the first and third Saturdays and the local legislator the second and fourth — under a calendar meant to cover all taluks and hoblis in 3–4 months.

Chief Minister D.K. Shivakumar’s Prajaseva Abhiyan will launch at Kudligi in Vijayanagara district on September 5, with taluk and hobli meetings every Saturday — the district-in-charge Minister chairing the first and third Saturdays and the local legislator the second and fourth — under a calendar meant to cover all taluks and hoblis in 3–4 months. #### Rule changes in Thimmappaiah Memorial tournament

**In short:** The KSCA-Dr. (Capt.) K.

The KSCA-Dr. (Capt.) K. Thimmappaiah Memorial Tournament beginning Tuesday will test an over limit of 120 overs in the first innings and 60 in the second; if the side batting fourth fails to reach the target in 60 overs it will be deemed to have lost rather than drawn, KSCA Hon. Secretary Santosh Menon said. Unused overs if a side is bowled out before 120 will carry 50% forward to the second innings, and a slow over-rate penalty will apply.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Rule changes in Thimmappaiah Memorial tournament
- **Told:** The KSCA-Dr.
- **Purpose:** Unused overs if a side is bowled out before 120 will carry 50% forward to the second innings, and a slow over-rate penalty will apply.

### India

#### Q1 GDP grows at 7.8%, defies war impact

**In short:** India's GDP expanded by 7.8% in the first quarter of the current financial year, driven by robust growth in services and manufacturing despite global disruptions from the West Asia conflict, according to official data released on Monday. The April-June growth is slower than the previous quarter's 8.6% expansion but higher than the 6.9% recorded in Q1 of 2025-26.

#### SC: Won't stop Sept 5 CJP march; trust it will be peaceful

**In short:** The Supreme Court on Monday declined to restrain the Cockroach Janta Party from staging a protest march in Delhi on September 5, with a bench of Chief Justice of India Surya Kant and Justices Joymalya Bagchi and V Mohana expressing confidence the demonstration would remain peaceful. Advocate Rizwan Ahmed argued the demand was not urgent and cited police raids and international media presence; the bench said counsel could file an application if any untoward incident occurred and directed that the matter be considered by the police, referring broader issues to a High-Powered Committee.

#### 14% monsoon shortfall so far, Sept too will be deficient: IMD

**In short:** Three months into the southwest monsoon season, all southern states remain severely rain deficient with little hope of recovery in September, which is likely to receive below-normal showers, the India Meteorological Department said on Monday, adding that August was the hottest month on record since 1901. Karnataka has a cumulative shortfall of 23%, Andhra Pradesh tops the list at 42%, Kerala and Tamil Nadu are both at 21%, Telangana at 19%, and the national deficiency is 14%, with September rainfall expected at 91% of the 167.9 mm average.

#### Minor girl gang-raped on moving bus in Noida

**In short:** Police said a 17-year-old Class 11 student from Mainpuri, Uttar Pradesh, was allegedly gang-raped by sleeper-bus driver Kuldeep and conductor Sandeep around 11.30 p.m. on August 4 during a journey from Pari Chowk in Greater Noida toward Sector 63 and then Outer Ring Road, after they offered her a lift; both have been arrested and the bus seized for forensic examination.

Police said a 17-year-old Class 11 student from Mainpuri, Uttar Pradesh, was allegedly gang-raped by sleeper-bus driver Kuldeep and conductor Sandeep around 11.30 p.m. on August 4 during a journey from Pari Chowk in Greater Noida toward Sector 63 and then Outer Ring Road, after they offered her a lift; both have been arrested and the bus seized for forensic examination. Deputy Commissioner of Police (North) Niharika Bhatt said she was later dropped near Kashmere Gate; the accused were traced to a bus parking lot in Timarpur. The Congress and AAP criticised the Delhi and Uttar Pradesh governments; NHRC member Priyank Kanoongo said notices had been issued to authorities.

#### Externment order can't be passed routinely, says Supreme Court

**In short:** The Supreme Court held that an externment order directly impinges on fundamental freedoms and cannot be passed as a matter of routine, and that multiple criminal cases alone do not justify externment. A bench of Justices Dipankar Datta and Sheel Nagu said the District Magistrate must have bona fide subjective satisfaction with objective material and a discernible nexus before issuing such an order.

The Supreme Court held that an externment order directly impinges on fundamental freedoms and cannot be passed as a matter of routine, and that multiple criminal cases alone do not justify externment. A bench of Justices Dipankar Datta and Sheel Nagu said the District Magistrate must have bona fide subjective satisfaction with objective material and a discernible nexus before issuing such an order. The court quashed the November 4, 2025 externment order against Vijay Kumar Rajpoot alias Viju by the Raigarh District Magistrate and faulted the Chhattisgarh High Court for a hands-off approach, allowing him to return to the districts from which he had been externed.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Externment order can't be passed routinely, says Supreme Court
- **Told:** The Supreme Court held that an externment order directly impinges on fundamental freedoms and cannot be passed as a matter of routine, and that multiple criminal cases alone do not justify externment.
- **Purpose:** The court quashed the November 4, 2025 externment order against Vijay Kumar Rajpoot alias Viju by the Raigarh District Magistrate and faulted the Chhattisgarh High Court for a hands-off approach, allowing him to return to the districts from which he had been externed.

**Important points**
- The court quashed the November 4, 2025 externment order against Vijay Kumar Rajpoot alias Viju by the Raigarh District Magistrate and faulted the Chhattisgarh High Court for a hands-off approach, allowing him to return to the districts from which he had been externed.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.3_

#### Indus water pact in force, rules Hague court; India rejects

**In short:** A World Bank-constituted court of arbitration ruled on Monday that the more than six-decade-old Indus Water Treaty between India and Pakistan remains fully in force despite New Delhi putting it in abeyance after the April 22, 2025 Pahalgam terror attack that killed 26 Indian citizens. India rejected the Hague-based award as an illegally constituted body with no jurisdiction over sovereign decisions, while the tribunal unanimously found no grounds for suspension or termination and barred India from concreting the dam wall and power intake of the 850-MW Ralte Hydroelectric Plant on the Chenab beyond certain levels until 90 days after the Neutral Expert's final decision, expected in July 2027.

#### India is 'vishwa guru', not a superpower: RSS chief Bhagwat

**In short:** RSS chief Mohan Bhagwat said India's civilisational traditions have always guided the principle of helping others without discrimination, and that the country has served the world as a 'vishwa guru' and not as a 'superpower'. Addressing the 'Hindu Vishwa Bandhu – Embrace the World in Friendship' event organised by Canadian Hindus for Outreach, Relations and Dialogue in Toronto on Sunday, he said the Hindu worldview was universal, rooted in 'Vasudhaiva Kutumbakam', and that Bharat extends help even without being asked.

#### SC: Real victim can settle for quashing criminal cases

**In short:** The Supreme Court ruled that the real victim of a crime—not necessarily the informant or complainant—is authorised to enter into a settlement that can form the basis for quashing criminal proceedings under the High Court's Section 482 inherent power, while compounding under Section 320 CrPC (now Section 359 BNSS) remains strictly statutory. A bench of Justices K V Vishwanathan and Arun Palli drew the distinction while hearing an appeal by Anand Kumar alias Sanjay Lalwani against a Madhya Pradesh High Court order refusing to quash a 2018 criminal case because the informant was not party to the compromise.

The Supreme Court ruled that the real victim of a crime—not necessarily the informant or complainant—is authorised to enter into a settlement that can form the basis for quashing criminal proceedings under the High Court's Section 482 inherent power, while compounding under Section 320 CrPC (now Section 359 BNSS) remains strictly statutory. A bench of Justices K V Vishwanathan and Arun Palli drew the distinction while hearing an appeal by Anand Kumar alias Sanjay Lalwani against a Madhya Pradesh High Court order refusing to quash a 2018 criminal case because the informant was not party to the compromise.

#### With just Rs 3 in account, Rs 4-cr cheque to Ram Temple returned

**In short:** A Chennai woman's cheque of Rs 4.1 crore donated to the Ram Temple in Ayodhya was returned after the bank reportedly found her account balance was just Rs 3; the Coimbatore resident had also donated Rs 10,000 in cash. Reports said she issued the cheque to increase wages of temple trust employees but asked for a written assurance on use of funds, which the trust did not oblige; the 2020 donation campaign raised Rs 3,500 crore, with cheques worth Rs 100 crore later returned on technical grounds.

#### Fadnavis dubs CJP 'Congress Jitao Programme'

**In short:** Maharashtra Chief Minister Devendra Fadnavis on Monday dubbed the Cockroach Janta Party as "Congress Jitao Programme" and accused the Opposition of forcing political contests over isolated issues after failing to defeat the BJP ideologically, also terming Rahul Gandhi's "Chhatron Ki Goonj" outreach as "Jhooth Ki Goonj." Addressing the BJP's state-level convention in Pune, Fadnavis said the Opposition had taken up issues including Ayodhya Ram Temple donation theft and Zilla Parishad school conditions, and commented on the Pune debate over loudspeakers during Ganeshotsav.

#### Over 6 crore names deleted so far in third phase of SIR

**In short:** As the third phase of the Special Intensive Revision of electoral rolls in 16 States and three Union Territories nears its end, more than 6 crore names have been deleted as per draft lists: of 36.08 crore names, 29.93 crore have been retained and 6.15 crore, or 17.04%, removed, though final numbers could differ after claims and objections; drafts are out except Nagaland (September 20) and Tripura (October 21), with Delhi and Maharashtra published on Monday. Delhi has the highest deletions in percentage terms at 32.78%; Maharashtra saw the most names removed at 2.06 crore, followed by Karnataka at 1.07 crore, then Telangana 73,39,235, Andhra Pradesh 44,89,512, Jharkhand 43,61,987, Haryana 33.83 lakh, Punjab 20,66,635, and Odisha 20,12,557.

As the third phase of the Special Intensive Revision of electoral rolls in 16 States and three Union Territories nears its end, more than 6 crore names have been deleted as per draft lists: of 36.08 crore names, 29.93 crore have been retained and 6.15 crore, or 17.04%, removed, though final numbers could differ after claims and objections; drafts are out except Nagaland (September 20) and Tripura (October 21), with Delhi and Maharashtra published on Monday. Delhi has the highest deletions in percentage terms at 32.78%; Maharashtra saw the most names removed at 2.06 crore, followed by Karnataka at 1.07 crore, then Telangana 73,39,235, Andhra Pradesh 44,89,512, Jharkhand 43,61,987, Haryana 33.83 lakh, Punjab 20,66,635, and Odisha 20,12,557. Final rolls start September 6 with Mizoram, Sikkim and Manipur first; Karnataka Chief Minister D.K. Shivakumar wrote to CEC Gyanesh Kumar calling the deletions “massive and unjustified” and urging an extension of the claims window, and the Telangana Chief Minister has done the same.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Over 6 crore names deleted so far in third phase of SIR

- **Purpose:** Shivakumar wrote to CEC Gyanesh Kumar calling the deletions “massive and unjustified” and urging an extension of the claims window, and the Telangana Chief Minister has done the same.

**Important points**
- Final rolls start September 6 with Mizoram, Sikkim and Manipur first.
- Karnataka Chief Minister D.K.
- Shivakumar wrote to CEC Gyanesh Kumar calling the deletions “massive and unjustified” and urging an extension of the claims window, and the Telangana Chief Minister has done the same.
